## 🧭 Reflow Risk (WCAG 1.4.10)

Page-level indicator: resizes to 320 CSS pixels wide and checks for horizontal overflow. This is a risk indicator, not a conformance verdict — see [Behavioral Accessibility Automation](https://github.com/mgifford/ACCESSIBILITY.md/blob/main/examples/BEHAVIORAL_ACCESSIBILITY_AUTOMATION.md) for what it can and cannot detect.

## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 18 page(s) - 18 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [list](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.12/list?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**Issue**: <ul> and <ol> must only directly contain <li>, <script> or <template> elements

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<ul class="social-media-menu__list">
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
.social-media-menu > .social-media-menu__list
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('.social-media-menu > .social-media-menu__list')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://www.portofrotterdam.com/nl/portlantis/wat-is-er-te-doen/lichtjestours
- https://www.portofrotterdam.com/en/port-future/energy-transition/pillar-2-new-energy-system
- https://www.portofrotterdam.com/de/pressebereich/videos
- https://www.portofrotterdam.com/en/portlantis/frequently-asked-questions
- https://www.portofrotterdam.com/de/binnenschifffahrt/verwenden-sie-ais-daten-der-binnenschifffahrt/einwilligung-fuer-die
- *...and 13 more page(s)*

---

#### Pattern 2: Affects 15 page(s) - 15 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [list](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.12/list?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**Issue**: <ul> and <ol> must only directly contain <li>, <script> or <template> elements

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<ul>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
.footer-directly-to-menu > ul
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser

## Detailed Failure Information (ALFA)

### https://www.portofrotterdam.com/nl/portlantis/wat-is-er-te-doen/lichtjestours

#### Rule: [SIA-R111: Interactive elements have a sufficient target size](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111)
**WCAG**: [SC 2.5.5](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/) (Level AAA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-09-14
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<button id="js-toggle-portlantis-menu" class="portlantis-menu-btn portlantis-menu-btn--transparent" type="button" aria-label="Menu openen" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="js-portlantis-side-menu" data-label-open="Menu openen" data-label-close="Menu sluiten">...</button>`
- XPath: `/button[@id="js-toggle-portlantis-menu"]`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-09-14
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a href="/nl/portlantis">Home</a>`
- XPath: `/a`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-09-14
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a class="portlantis-menu-link portlantis-menu-link__portlantis-footer  " href="/nl/portlantis/contact" title="Contact" target="_self">...</a>`
- XPath: `/a[@class="portlantis-menu-link portlantis-menu-link__portlantis-footer  "]`

#### Rule: [SIA-R53: Headings do not skip heading levels in the document outline](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r53)
**WCAG**: Best Practice

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-09-14
- Message: The heading skips one or more levels
- HTML: `<h4>Op vrijdagavond: 3-gangendiner: € 32,50</h4>`
- XPath: `/h4`

#### Rule: [SIA-R64: Heading elements have a non-empty accessible name](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r64)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.3.1](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/info-and-relationships) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-09-14
- Message: The heading does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<h2 />`
- XPath: `/h2`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-09-14
- Message: The heading does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<h1 class="hero__title hero__title--without-background-img" />`
- XPath: `/h1[@class="hero__title hero__title--without-background-img"]`

#### Rule: [SIA-R66: Text has enhanced contrast with its background](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r66)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.6](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-enhanced) (Level AAA)

**Failure 1:**
